HerkunftS4-symmetric elimination of a six-node orbit from CA-T3-37-2670-PI-MPC. A seeded random order selected the same orbit identified in an independent A4 search. Its mass was transferred to the closest remaining orbit before bounded Levenberg–Marquardt.
